6. Recommended for M3 (not gating)

  1. Calibration UX. Home's and onboarding's Calibrate open a separate AR screen whose calibration is lost on leaving. After Use this calibration, continue straight into measuring with a visible "calibrated for this session" banner, or start calibration from inside Measure only. Also drop the calibration on a tracking reset after a pause: the world frame can shift, which the plane check only partly catches.
  2. Bench test (PLAN §7) with the fixed APK. Results from the fbe1479 APK understate error and must be discarded. Tune AIM_PIXELS_VGA, the tier floors, CONSISTENT_ABS/REL, the badge thresholds and the calibration acceptance from the data. Re-enable card/A4 only if the data justifies a smaller floor.
  3. M3 Room migration. Add the canonical-record columns (device tier, ARCore version, calibration factor and scope, raw lengths) and run SpaceViewMigrationTest on a device.
  4. Onboarding copy says "1 m or more", while the tip and known-length detail say "2 m or more works best" (1 m is usually rejected). Align on 2 m.
  5. Target-distance pill in imperial shows whole inches (MeasureFormatter.targetDistance). Make it as coarse as the metric 0.1 m, for example to the half-foot.
  6. Per-frame allocations (M2 minor 8) and the SceneView keep-rule narrowing plus unused materials and skybox (about 2.5 MB): M6, verified on a device.
  7. Settings writes use runCatching inside launch. Use the same guarded helper as the ViewModels (rethrow CancellationException) and surface failures.
  8. Create the GitHub remote and get a first green CI run. Lint (all modules plus lintRelease), the bundletool size gate and the 16 KB check have only been run locally or not at all.
Reviewsdocs/reviews/M3-gallery-review.md · updated 05 Nov 2026, 12:19

M3 review: AR Gallery, Room v2 and front-photo rectifier (uncommitted, on top of ccebb01)

Reviewer: Reviewer agent, 2026-11-05. Scope: the uncommitted gallery work (Builder B), excluding HANDOFF.md:

  • data/.../gallery/*, the SpaceViewDatabase v1→v2 migration, MeasurementEntity / MeasurementRepository, schemas/.../2.json
  • data/src/test/.../gallery/* and SpaceViewMigrationTest
  • app/.../gallery/* and app/.../scan/imaging/* with their tests
  • AppNavHost gallery routes, AppContainer, the manifest FileProvider, res/xml/file_paths.xml, and the EN/HI strings

Builder A's in-progress scan flow is out of scope. Source was not edited.

Verdict: 0 BLOCKER, 3 MAJOR, 18 MINOR. The storage layer is well built:

  • Staged writes with temp files, fsync and rename.
  • The row insert and the directory move happen in one transaction, and the rollback tests cover it.
  • The migration SQL matches the exported schema byte for byte.
  • The 0.5 MB budget is enforced against real encoder output.

The rectifier maths and the GLB orientation are correct. The problems are three:

  • TV diagonal ±. It is understated by 20–27%, and it is still shown when the diagonal rests on a typed value.
  • Dimension edits. They write the GLB before the database row, so the two can diverge.
  • Ad route. The gallery-item route will show the pending after-save interstitial when the user taps Place on the Saved screen, which the ad rules forbid.

How the findings were checked

  • Build lock taken, then released. Two test runs:

    • :data:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.spaceview.data.gallery.*: green.
    • :app:testDebugUnitTest --tests com.spaceview.app.gallery.* --tests com.spaceview.app.scan.imaging.*: green.

  • Sizes printed by the tests. The JDK JPEG encoder stands in for Android's; the thumbnail is JPEG rather than WebP, which overstates it.

    Case model.glb front.jpg thumb Total Share of 500 KB
    Typical 1024×768 103 448 B 100 896 B 7 373 B 211 717 B 42%
    Detailed poster 206 296 B 203 741 B 19 752 B 429 789 B 86%
    Worst-case noise (stepped down to q66, 768 px) 222 232 B 219 680 B 13 235 B 455 147 B 91%

    GLB overhead without the JPEG is 2 552 B, against the 4 096 B margin in estimatedItemBytes.

  • Rectifier timing: 1024×731 from a 1920×1440 frame takes 16–17 ms (desktop JVM).

  • Migration SQL. A script compared the gallery_items createSql in 2.json with the string in MIGRATION_1_2: identical. The identityHash in 2.json (1907359e…) matches the one in the generated SpaceViewDatabase_Impl, so the JSON was exported by Room, not written by hand.

  • Room 2.8.5 bytecode (room-runtime-android AAR, javap). In compatibility mode, withTransaction → startTransactionCoroutine is a suspendCancellableCoroutine that runs the block in runBlocking on the transaction executor. This is the basis of MINOR 1.

  • Diagonal uncertainty. Computed by hand against the engine's Shapes.rectangle model (MAJOR 1).

BLOCKER

None.

MAJOR

M1. The TV diagonal ± is 20–27% too small, and it is shown with a ± even when the diagonal rests on a typed value

Where

  • app/.../gallery/GalleryFormatter.kt:62-68: diagonal() propagates σ_W and σ_H as independent.
  • GalleryFormatter.kt:79-80: diagonalDetail().
  • GalleryViewModels.kt:123-125: the diagonal row.

What happens

  • On every non-LiDAR tier the stored σ_W and σ_H are dominated by the same VIO scale error (σ_s ≈ 1%). The engine knows this. Shapes.rectangle (engine Shapes.kt:286-290) propagates only the random parts and then applies the scale once to the diagonal.
  • The gallery recombines the stored totals as if they were independent: σ_d = √((w σ_w)² + (h σ_h)²) / d. This cancels part of the correlated scale term.
  • For an estimated W or H, measurement() gives σ = 0. The diagonal then takes the ± of the other side only. The row shows, for example, "43.0 in ± 0.3 (109 cm)" and "Estimated (typed, not measured)". A typed value ends up carrying a measured-looking ±, which is exactly what the edit rules forbid.

Failure scenario (NO_DEPTH, σ_s = 1.02%, 2 mm random per side; ±95)

Item Engine Gallery shows Under by
43″ TV, 0.952 × 0.536 m ±22.2 mm (0.87 in) ±17.8 mm (0.70 in) 20%
4:3 monitor, 0.60 × 0.45 m ±15.5 mm ±11.7 mm 25%
Square panel ±14.7 mm ±10.7 mm 27%

The M2 review (M1) already established that the engine's coverage is tuned to just over 95%, so a 20% shrink drops the band the user reads well below 95%.

In the second case, the user edits the TV width to the box label's 95 cm. The spec block still shows the diagonal "± 0.3 in", which is no longer a measurement of anything.

Fix

  • Carry the engine's diagonal in the contract. Add diagonal: Dimension? to ScanDraft and a diagonal_bound95_m column. v2 has not shipped, so 2.json can still change without a migration.
  • On edit, recompute it conservatively: bound95_d = (w·b_w + h·b_h) / d. This is the fully-correlated bound, ≥ the engine value in all three cases above (22.5 / 16.0 / 15.2 mm).
  • When W or H is an estimate, show the diagonal without a ± (σ = 0, "~"), as diagonalInches already does on the card.
  • Add a test: when either side is an estimate, the diagonal row has no plusMinus; when neither is, the diagonal ± ≥ the fully-correlated bound.

M2. updateDimensions writes the new GLB before the database row, so the model and the row can disagree

Where: data/.../gallery/GalleryRepository.kt:205-219. writeAtomic(model, glb) (210) replaces model.glb in place, then dao.setDimensions(...) (211-219) runs outside any transaction. The KDoc (186-191) promises "the item is then unchanged" on failure.

What happens: each of these leaves model.glb at the new size while the row (and every number on screen) keeps the old size:

  • setDimensions throws (SQLiteFull, I/O error, or the DB closed on a corrupt file). guarded returns false and the user sees "Couldn't save the change".
  • The process dies between the rename and the UPDATE (low-memory kill while the dialog closes).

Nothing ever reconciles the two. The KDoc on GalleryItem.modelFile says the GLB is "true-size", and M4 places that GLB at 1:1 against a wall. The user then gets a frame placed at 50 cm while the spec block says 45.7 cm ± 0.5. A wrong true-size placement is the failure this app exists to avoid.

Fix (any one of these)

  1. Version the model file. Write model-<updatedAt>.glb. Update model_path together with the dimensions in one UPDATE (or one withTransaction). Delete the old file after the commit. A crash then leaves either the old row with the old file, or an unreferenced new file that cleanUp removes.
  2. Treat the row as the source of truth. When M4 loads a model, compare the GLB extras.widthM/heightM/depthM with the row and regenerate it if they differ (it takes milliseconds). Also do this check in cleanUp.

Add a test with a DAO whose setDimensions throws: the GLB extras must still equal the row.

M3. The gallery-item route uses up the after-save interstitial, so Place on the Saved screen will show an ad

Where

  • app/.../nav/AppNavHost.kt:156: NonArScreenAdEffect(ads) on GALLERY_ITEM_PATTERN.
  • ads/AdsManager.kt:270-283 and MeasureVisitTracker.consumePending() (MeasureVisitTracker.kt:64-68).

What happens

  • NonArScreenAdEffect makes any non-AR destination that reaches RESUMED use up a pending after-measure chance, then show the interstitial if AdPolicy allows it.
  • HANDOFF (M3 plan) wires the scan flow's Saved screen as [Place → gallery item | Share | Done (ad slot)]. Until M4, Place lands on this route.
  • When the scan visit ends with a save, leaving it arms the pending chance (as Measure does with onMeasureLeaving).
  • The first non-AR screen to resume is then the gallery item, and it shows the ad.

Failure scenario: a user past the grace period scans a frame, saves it and taps Place in my room. The interstitial opens over the item screen. ui-ux §5.2 (eligibility rule 5, and the flow diagram: "[Place in my room] → AR (NO AD)") and Play's disruptive-ads policy both call this unexpected: the user tapped a button expecting to start an activity. The same happens if the Saved screen's Share is implemented as "go to item, then share".

This depends on how Builder A wires the Saved screen, but the gallery route in scope is what makes it fire.

Fix

  • Give NonArScreenAdEffect a consumePending: Boolean = true parameter and pass false on GALLERY_ITEM. Alternatively, have the scan flow call visits.dropPending() (new) before navigating for Place or Share.
  • Implement §5.2 rule 6 (carry the chance over to the next natural exit, once) only if the product wants it.
  • Add an AdPolicy / MeasureVisitTracker unit test: "leave the scan visit via Place → no ad", and include it in Builder A's review.

MINOR

  1. A cancelled save() cleans up while the Room transaction is still running. GalleryRepository.kt:142-177. Room's compat withTransaction resumes the caller with CancellationException as soon as its Job is cancelled, while the block keeps running on the transaction thread until its next suspension point (verified in the 2.8.5 bytecode). This causes three problems:

    • The catch (173-177) can delete committedDir, which is a plain captured var with no memory barrier, after setFiles returned but before the commit. The result is a committed row with no files.
    • writeLock is released early, so a concurrent cleanUp() can delete the not-yet-committed <id> directory.
    • The caller sees "not saved" while the item exists.

    The window is milliseconds; it is triggered by Back during Save. Fix: ensureActive() after encoding, then run the stage → transaction → return part in withContext(NonCancellable), so cancellation never lands between the move and the commit.

  2. Durability gaps, and the startup sweep is incomplete. GalleryRepository.kt:162, 245-262, 401-417.

    • The directory rename and the new directory entries are never fsynced. On power loss, the WAL commit can survive while the rename does not, leaving a row that points at a missing gallery/<id>/ (cleanUp then deletes the .tmp-… staging directory that held the files).
    • cleanUp does not handle rows whose files are missing (android-performance.md §storage: "deletes orphan directories and rows whose files are missing").
    • A model.glb.tmp left inside an item directory by an interrupted edit is never removed, and dirBytes counts it.

    Fix: fsync the parent directory after the rename (open it read-only and call FileChannel.force, or accept the risk and document it). In cleanUp, flag rows whose model_path file is missing (regenerate the GLB from the row, since it is pure data), and delete stray *.tmp files.

  3. A "measured" dimension with bound95 = 0 is accepted and shown with no ± and no estimate label. GalleryRepository.kt:381-384 (validate allows ≥ 0); GalleryFormatter.kt:55. If the scan flow ever passes a measured side with a zero bound, the spec block shows "45.7 cm" at the finest step, unmarked, which breaks "every number carries its tolerance". Fix: in save, require bound95 > 0 when !isEstimate.

  4. Provenance is a single boolean, and edits throw away the measured value.

    • ScanDraft.kt:5, strings.xml:224-225, GalleryRepository.kt:186-222.
    • isEstimate mixes a scan-flow preset (for example a frame's default depth) with a user-typed value. A preset depth is labelled "Estimated (typed, not measured)", which is false. An item whose sides are all presets shows "Edited by you".
    • An edit overwrites the measured value and its ±, so ui-ux §4.4's "the original measured value is kept" and "Reset to measured" cannot be built later without data that is already gone.

    Fix (cheap now that v2 has not shipped): replace the flag with source: MEASURED | PRESET | TYPED. Add measured_*_m / measured_*_bound95_m columns, set on save and never overwritten. Change the copy to "Estimated (not measured)" or make it depend on the source.

  5. The canonical-record columns are added but never filled. A calibrated History row records calibration_factor = 1.0, which the KDoc defines as "uncalibrated".

    • MeasureViewModel.kt:105-115 does not pass deviceTier, arcoreVersion, calibrationFactor or rawPrimaryMetres.
    • MeasurementEntity.kt:45.
    • The gallery entity has calibrated but no factor, scope or raw lengths, which the M2 gate §6 item 3 asks for.

    Fix: wire the four values from the Reading / MeasureModel. Make calibration_factor nullable (NULL = unknown for legacy rows). Add factor and raw dims to gallery_items before v2 freezes.

  6. The budget is not a hard limit. GalleryRepository.kt:278-291 returns the last attempt even when it is still over, and save never checks bytes <= MAX_SCAN_BYTES. The step-down measures real encoder bytes, so this holds in practice (worst case 455 KB above). Still, detailed scans use 86–91% of the budget, and the GLB overhead is an estimate. Fix: after dirBytes(stage), if the total is over budget, re-encode one more step (512 px at q50) or fail loudly in debug builds. Add a test with an 80-character Devanagari label (the JSON names grow by about 480 B).

  7. CPU-heavy encoding runs on Dispatchers.IO with no parallelism cap. GalleryRepository.kt:94 (and the rectifier KDoc suggests Dispatchers.Default). The worst case is up to 12 JPEG encodes plus a WebP encode, and it may run while the scan route's ARCore session is still alive. android-performance.md recommends Dispatchers.Default.limitedParallelism(2) for JPEG/WebP/GLB work so ARCore's VIO threads are not starved ("VIO frequency low"). Fix: take a separate cpu dispatcher for encodeImages and buildGlb, and keep io for files and Room.

  8. The thumbnail aliases. GalleryImageCodec.kt:68: createScaledBitmap(..., filter = true) is a single bilinear pass, and 1024 → 256 is a 4:1 minification. Fine text and stripes on posters will moiré in the grid. Fix: halve in steps (1024 → 512 → 256), or decode the thumbnail separately with inSampleSize = 4.

  9. Rectifier: an isotropic prefilter on anisotropic quads, and an unbounded source window. RectifyMath.kt:47-51, FrontPhotoRectifier.kt:38-39.

    • prefilter comes from the longest edge only. A strongly foreshortened face (3000 px wide, 300 px tall in the photo, true aspect 1:1) gets a 2× box filter in both directions. The short direction drops to 150 px before it is upsampled to 1024, which blurs it for no reason.
    • The IntArray window covers the quad's bounding box. With a 12 MP still that is about 48 MB plus the downsampled copy, an OOM risk on 3–4 GB phones.

    Fix: use separate x and y prefilter factors (from the max edge lengths in each direction). Document in FrontPhotoRectifier that the source must be the ARCore CPU image or a still capped at about 2–3 MP, and tell Builder A.

  10. Share copies outlive delete; the previous copy is deleted on the next share; getUriForFile sits outside the try. GalleryImages.kt:99-131, GalleryRepository.kt:225-234.

    • Deleting an item does not remove its cache/share/SpaceView-*.jpg, while the dialog promises "Its photo … will be removed from this phone" (strings.xml:244).
    • listFiles().delete() on the next share can break a receiver that reads lazily (a Gmail draft, a slow upload).
    • FileProvider.getUriForFile (112) can throw IllegalArgumentException outside the try, in a rememberCoroutineScope launch, which crashes the app.

    Fix: clear cache/share in delete(). Name the copy per share (<id>-<updatedAt>.jpg) and prune copies older than about 1 day instead of all of them. Move getUriForFile inside the try.

  11. The FileProvider exposes more than the share needs. res/xml/file_paths.xml:5: files-path gallery/ is never used (shares go through the cache/share/ copy). The provider is not exported and grants are per URI, so this is least-privilege hygiene. Fix: remove it until M4 or M5 actually shares a GLB.

  12. Bad rows disappear silently, and a storage error looks like an empty gallery. GalleryRepository.kt:309-328, 101-105.

    • toDomain() returns null for any unknown enum name, for example after a DeviceTier rename or a downgrade. The item becomes invisible and cannot be deleted, while its files still count in totalBytes.
    • observeAll().catch { emit(emptyList()) } makes the grid show "Nothing saved yet", and the flow ends, so it never recovers until the screen is resubscribed.

    Fix: map unknown names to a fallback (OTHER, DeviceTier.NO_DEPTH, and the worst Quality) rather than dropping the row. Expose an error state ("Couldn't open your gallery") instead of the empty state.

  13. A late delete callback can pop the Gallery too. GalleryViewModels.kt:110-114, GalleryItemScreen.kt:144. onDeleted = onBack = popBackStack() runs whenever the delete coroutine finishes. If the user presses Back first and the coroutine finishes during the exit transition (before the ViewModel is cleared), a second pop takes them out of the Gallery. Fix: guard it as the Measure route does (currentBackStackEntry?.id == entry.id), or emit a one-shot Deleted event that the screen collects.

  14. The TV diagonal ignores the unit preference on the card and in share text. GalleryFormatter.kt:71-76, GalleryItemScreen.kt:174-175. diagonalInches always formats inches; the detail row respects the preference. A METRIC user sees "43.0 in diagonal" only. ui-ux §6.4 says the defaults are "all switchable". Fix: under METRIC show cm first with inches secondary ("109 cm (43 in)", Croma style). Keep inches under AUTO.

  15. Accessibility and i18n details.

    • GalleryScreen.kt:155: the compact dimensions are read by TalkBack as "18 times 24 times tilde 1 in". The "~" estimate marker has no spoken meaning and nothing on the card says "estimated". Add a contentDescription built from the same data ("about 1 inch, estimated").
    • GalleryFormatter.kt:115-122: Devanagari digits (४५), which some Hindi keyboards type, fail to parse and get the misleading "Enter a size from 1 mm to 50 m". Normalise Unicode digits (Character.digit) before parsing.

  16. The gallery banner position differs from ui-ux §5.3. GalleryScreen.kt:100-105. The research suggests an inline full-width row every 12 items, or an anchored banner at the top under the title. AdMob specifically flags banners next to gallery images, and this one is anchored right under a scrolling thumbnail grid (24 dp gap). Being outside the lazy list and anchored is fine (no reload on scroll, which addresses M2 minor 10). Fix: move it to the top, or add a divider and keep at least 24 dp. Record the decision in ui-ux.md.

  17. Edit dialog state. GalleryItemScreen.kt:322: typed uses remember while dialog uses rememberSaveable. After process death or recreation the dialog reopens with the measured values and the typed numbers are lost. The "was this field really edited" rule (377-383) lives inside a composable, so no test covers it. Fix: rememberSaveable with a list saver, and move edited() into GalleryFormatter with unit tests (unit switch, retyping the prefilled value).

  18. The empty state never offers "Scan an object". AppNavHost.kt:145-150 does not pass onScan, so the button stays hidden. The copy says "Scan an object to save it here." with no way to do it. The home_scan_object* / home_gallery* strings are unused until the orchestrator wires Home. Fix: wire onScan when Builder A's SCAN route lands.

Missing tests

  • The migration test has never run. SpaceViewMigrationTest needs a device; the PC has no emulator, and .github/workflows/android.yml runs no connected tests. M2 gate m12 carried this over. Only the SQL comparison in this review checks the migration. Add: run it on the bench phone before the M3 gate (add it to docs/TESTING.md), or add a Robolectric JVM variant.
  • Real DAO and transaction. GalleryDao SQL (the setDimensions bindings, ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC) and RoomGalleryTransactions are never executed: all repository tests use FakeGalleryDao with FakeTransactions. Add: an instrumented DAO test (same device run), or Robolectric with an in-memory Room.
  • updateDimensions failure: a DAO whose setDimensions throws (M2).
  • Save cancellation: cancel during and after the transaction; there must be no row without files and no files without a row (MINOR 1).
  • Diagonal honesty: no ± on the diagonal when W or H is an estimate; diagonal ± ≥ the correlated bound (M1).
  • Ads: leaving the scan visit via Place or Share shows no interstitial (M3).
  • Budget: an 80-character Devanagari label and a 16:1 aspect photo (MINOR 6).
  • End-to-end orientation: rectify a synthetic photo whose top-left is red, encode it, build the GLB, and check that the texel at the front face's UV (0, 0) is red. Each piece is tested on its own; the chain is not.
  • Android-only paths: FrontPhotoRectifier (HARDWARE bitmap copy and recycle), AndroidGalleryImageCodec, and the FileProvider share intent. These go on the device checklist.

Checked and OK

  • Migration.
    • MIGRATION_1_2 creates gallery_items and its index exactly as in 2.json, and adds four nullable or defaulted columns to measurements. It never touches existing rows.
    • No fallbackToDestructiveMigration anywhere.
    • calibration_factor's DEFAULT 1.0 matches the entity's defaultValue = "1.0", so Room's validation passes.
    • SpaceViewDatabase.get() makes History and Gallery share one instance, so invalidations propagate.
    • The migration test asserts the legacy row's defaults.
  • Save atomicity (normal paths).
    • The staging directory, each file written as temp + fsync + rename, then insert, move and setFiles in one transaction.
    • Rollback deletes the staging directory and the moved directory.
    • A reused id after a rollback overwrites the orphan directory.
    • cleanUp removes staging and orphan directories under the write lock.
    • Tests cover a DB failure, a failure after the move, a codec failure, invalid drafts and id reuse.
  • Delete removes the row, then gallery/<id>/. A failed file delete is swept later. Tested.
  • Edits (data rules).
    • Each edited side becomes isEstimate = true, bound95 = 0. Untouched sides keep their measured value and ±.
    • The badge never improves (Quality.worst). When all sides are typed, the badge is replaced by "Edited by you".
    • The UI sends only fields whose text differs from the prefilled value.
    • The GLB is regenerated with the same embedded photo; this is tested by searching the GLB bytes for the photo.
    • After a width correction the front texture is stretched to the new W:H. That is correct: the rectifier mapped the object's real corners, so the corrected aspect is the true one.
  • Budget (PLAN M3 gate): holds, with the numbers in the table above.
    • The step-down measures actual encoder bytes, so it carries over to Android's encoder.
    • WebP thumbnails will be smaller than the JPEG stand-in.
    • The photo stays within ui-ux's 300 KB guidance for shared images.
  • GLB.
    • 1 unit = 1 m; +Y up; front face +Z, with the front-face top-left vertex at UV (0,0), which glTF defines as the image's top-left, i.e. JPEG row 0. The texture is not mirrored.
    • FRAME/TV use BACK_CENTER (box spans z ∈ [0, d] from the wall); other kinds use BOTTOM_CENTER.
    • Accessor min/max bounds are correct.
    • The JPEG is embedded as image/jpeg (gltfio has no WebP support).
  • Rectifier maths.
    • The pixel-centre convention is consistent: output centres at (i + ½, j + ½) map through H⁻¹, then −½ gives sample indices.
    • Prefilter coordinates are scaled by 1/s relative to the crop origin.
    • Fixed-point bilinear weights sum to 65536, with no Int overflow.
    • NaN and out-of-range samples are clamped.
    • Counter-clockwise corners are reordered, so the texture is not mirrored. Rotating the camera image by 90° keeps the winding, so a landscape sensor image works as long as corner 0 is the object's top-left as seen upright. Contract for Builder A: corners in source-image pixels, corner 0 = top-left as seen by the user, and aspect = measured W/H.
    • The output long side is the quad's longest edge, clamped to [16, 1024]. The output is never upscaled beyond the photo's detail.
    • 10 tests pass, including perspective round-trip, CCW = CW, prefilter alignment and degenerate quads.
  • Honest display.
    • Estimates format with σ = 0 and a "~" marker.
    • maxPlusMinus ignores estimates and rounds up through the M2-fixed Units.
    • The spec block shows ± per measured side plus "Estimated" per typed side, and "The rating covers only the measured sizes" when mixed.
    • Share text carries the ± and the secondary unit.
    • Units follow the setting (frames read in inches, objects in cm), apart from MINOR 14.
  • Threading.
    • All repository work goes through withContext(io); image decode in GalleryImage runs on IO.
    • guarded / guardedOrNull and the Flow catch rethrow CancellationException. Every runCatching wraps non-suspending code only.
    • Bitmaps in the codec are recycled on every path.
    • Grid thumbnails are 256 px, keyed by id, cached in a 12 MB LRU, and formatted outside composition.
    • The ViewModels hold no Context or Activity.
  • Security and privacy.
    • The provider is exported=false with grantUriPermissions.
    • The share intent sets only FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION with ClipData.
    • No storage permission is added.
    • The share file name is sanitised; StorageLayout.itemDir rejects path separators.
  • Ads.
    • No banner on the item screen (stricter than §5.3 allows).
    • Nothing near Share or Delete.
    • The grid banner sits outside the lazy grid, so it does not reload on scroll.
    • No ads on AR routes.
  • i18n. EN and HI have identical key sets (only app_name is EN-only). No count strings, so no plurals are needed. Numbers are formatted with Locale.ROOT (Western digits, as ui-ux §6.4 asks). Every new string has a Hindi translation.
  • Accessibility.
    • 48–56 dp touch targets; title headings marked as headings.
    • The detail photo has a content description; card thumbnails are decorative inside a clickable card with its title.
    • The badge carries its own semantics.
Reviewsdocs/reviews/M3-scan-review.md · updated 05 Nov 2026, 12:56

M3 review: scan capture flow (Builder A, uncommitted, on top of ccebb01)

Reviewer: Reviewer agent, 2026-11-05. Scope: Builder A's M3 files only.

  • :ar: the new ScanComposer, ImageProjection, FrontCaptureGate and Nv21, plus changes to MeasurementComposer (BOX), MeasureSession (scan and front-capture API), FrameReaders and DisplayedBoundTest.
  • app/scan: ScanRoute, ScanArScreen, ScanReviewScreen, ScanViewModel and ScanDraftBuilder.
  • Changed in app: measure/*, ads/MeasureVisitTracker and AdsManager (leaveWithoutAd), nav/AppNavHost (SCAN), HomeScreen, and the strings.

Builder B's gallery, data and imaging code is reviewed in M3-gallery-review.md. Here I only checked how A calls it.

No source was edited.

Verdict: 1 BLOCKER, 2 MAJOR, 16 MINOR.

The measurement side is honest:

  • BOX W × D comes from the rectangle path, with its geometry checks and tier floor.
  • H comes from the height path, with its own calibration scope.
  • Every ± is rounded up.
  • Calibration cannot reach a scan.
  • Edits become TYPED estimates with no ±.

The ads wiring meets ui-ux §5.2. The Measure flow is not regressed.

The problems are all in the front photo:

  • The auto-capture never fires on a real phone, because SceneView delivers repeated frames.
  • For a box, the face that is photographed can differ from the face whose W × H the outline reported.
  • After a pause, the capture projects corners that are no longer anchored.

How the findings were checked

  • Reading. I read every file in scope (git diff, plus the new files). I also read the unchanged code each one depends on: MeasureSession lifecycle, SessionSetup, Shapes.rectangle and its build step, Homography.rectify, FrontPhotoRectifier/RectifyMath, GalleryRepository.save/shareCopy, and ScanDraft.
  • SceneView 4.65.0 bytecode (javap on the cached AAR):
    • ARSceneKt$ARSceneView$43$1 drives the frame loop with withFrameNanos, so it runs at display rate.
    • Each tick it calls ARSession.updateOrNull(), which only drops frames with timestamp 0, then onARFrame, then notifyHostOfFrame.
    • notifyHostOfFrame calls onSessionUpdated unconditionally. The new-frame flag (lastTs.getAndSet(ts) != ts) only feeds FrameRateGate.shouldRender.
    • SpaceView configures UpdateMode.LATEST_CAMERA_IMAGE (SessionSetup.kt:82). With a 30 fps camera on a display of 60 Hz or more, at least half of the onFrame calls therefore repeat the previous frame's timestamp. M2's TrackingGate.kt:161 (dt <= 1e-4 → return) and FrameReaders.pointCloud already allow for this.
  • Probe test (ar/src/test/.../TmpScanReviewProbeTest.kt, deleted after the run; lock taken and released):
Probe Result
FrontCaptureGate, steady and parallel (5°), face inside, 30 fps camera fed at 60 Hz (each timestamp twice), 10 s captured = false, hints HOLD_STILL=301, HOLDING=299 (alternating every call)
ScanComposer.frontEdge, wardrobe 2.0 × 0.6 m, camera at (1.0, 1.4, 1.0) edge 0 (long front) ✓
same, camera at (1.6, 1.4, 0.6) edge 0 ✓
same, camera at (1.8, 1.4, 0.4), i.e. in front of the long face, 20 cm from its end edge 1 (short side) ✗
same, camera at (1.9, 1.4, 0.5) edge 1 ✗
  • Unit tests in the current tree, all green:
    • :ar:testDebugUnitTest: 70 tests.
    • :app scan.* and ads.*: 57 tests, of which ScanDraftBuilder 12, RectifyMath 12 and MeasureVisitTracker 14.

BLOCKER

B1. The front photo is never taken: repeated frames reset the hold every other call

Where

  • ar/.../FrontCaptureGate.kt:108-117: if (prev == null || dt <= 0 || dt > MAX_GAP_S) { angular = NaN; linear = NaN }.
  • FrontCaptureGate.kt:88,91-93: !isSteady() gives HOLD_STILL, which sets holdingSinceNs = -1.
  • ar/.../MeasureSession.kt:400,740-752: the gate is fed on every onFrame, with no timestamp check.

What happens

  • onSessionUpdated runs once per display vsync, and in LATEST_CAMERA_IMAGE mode a call without a new camera image returns the same Frame timestamp (see above).
  • On each repeated frame dt == 0, so the motion estimate becomes NaN. The gate then reports HOLD_STILL and restarts the 600 ms hold.
  • The next real frame starts the hold again, and the repeat after it resets it. held >= HOLD_NS is never reached.

Failure scenario

  • On any phone with a 60/90/120 Hz display, step 3 never captures. The probe shows this over 10 s of a perfectly still, parallel hold.
  • The card flickers between "Hold still." and "Hold still. Taking the photo…" about 30 times a second, which also makes frontCapture emit every frame.
  • The text is in a polite live region, so TalkBack keeps re-announcing it.
  • The user's only way forward is Skip photo, so no scan ever gets its front photo, which is the core of M3. The existing tests feed strictly increasing timestamps, so they pass.

Fix

  1. In MeasureSession.processFrontCapture, skip the gate when frame.timestamp == lastCaptureTs, and remember the last timestamp. This also halves the per-frame work.
  2. In FrontCaptureGate.updateMotion, treat dt <= 1e-4 like TrackingGate does: return without touching angular, linear, lastTs, lastPose or the hold.
  3. Add a test: a 30 fps camera fed at 60 Hz and at 120 Hz, with each timestamp repeated, captures within HOLD_NS + 3 camera frames.
  4. Add to the device checklist: "photo is auto-taken within about 1 s of holding still, on a 90/120 Hz phone".

MAJOR

M1. For a box, the photographed face is chosen separately from the outline's W/D. The rule picks the short side while the user stands in front of the long face.

Where

  • app/.../scan/ScanArScreen.kt:272: remember(finished) { outlineOf(finished) } → MeasureSession.scanOutline (:514-518). This decides W vs D with frontEdge(points, pose at that moment) (ScanComposer.kt:90-93).
  • MeasureSession.kt:531: startFrontCapture recomputes captureEdge with the pose at arming time, and that edge picks the photographed face (ScanComposer.kt:104-110,153-165).
  • ScanComposer.kt:116-129: frontEdge = the base edge whose midpoint is horizontally nearest the camera.
  • ScanViewModel.kt:142: the photo is warped to outline.width / outline.height.
  • Same root cause for a horizontal flat face (a poster on a table): faceUp (ScanComposer.kt:173-178) takes "up" from the camera direction once at outline time and again at every face refresh. W/H is fixed at outline time, but the photo's orientation follows wherever the user stands at capture.

What happens

  1. The edge-midpoint rule is wrong for long objects. In the probe, standing 0.4–0.5 m in front of a 2 m wardrobe, 10–20 cm inside its right end, selects the 0.6 m side.
  2. The decision is made twice, at different moments: when the reading finishes (and again whenever the reading changes as anchors refine), and when the photo step arms.
  3. If the two decisions differ:
    • The outline says W = front edge A.
    • The capture photographs face B, of width D.
    • The view model stretches face B's photo to A's W:H.
    • The gallery GLB puts the side panel on the front.

Failure scenario

  • A user taps the top of a 120 × 40 cm cabinet while standing near its front-right corner, which is the natural place to see the top.
  • The Outline card shows W 120, D 40.
  • After Next, startFrontCapture picks the side edge. The guidance says "Turn to face the object" until the user faces the 40 cm side panel.
  • The 40 × 85 cm panel is then warped into a 120 × 85 texture, a 3× horizontal stretch, and saved without warning.
  • If the user doesn't move, the Outline card can also swap W and D while it is on screen, because the outline is recomputed for each new reading.

Fix

  • Decide the front face once, when the user taps Next. Store it in ScanOutline (frontEdge: Int, and for FLAT the up vector used) and pass it to startFrontCapture(shape, outline). The capture then never re-decides.
  • Choose the edge by facing, not by midpoint distance. For example, take argmax over k of n_k · normalize(horizontal(camera − mid_k)), where n_k is the outward horizontal normal of edge k. That is the face the camera is most squarely in front of.
  • Tests:
    • the probe positions, which should all give edge 0;
    • an outline/capture round trip where the camera moves between Next and arming, and W/D and the face must not change;
    • a horizontal flat face captured from the other side, which must keep the W/H orientation.

M2. After a pause, the photo is taken against frozen, unanchored corners

Where

  • MeasureSession.kt:321-325: onSessionPaused detaches every anchor. A complete outline keeps its last positions, unanchored, by M2 design.
  • MeasureSession.kt:385-400: unanchored points are skipped, so allTracking stays true.
  • MeasureSession.kt:527-539: startFrontCapture does not check for unanchored points.
  • ScanArScreen.kt:142-148: the photo step re-arms automatically once sessionRunning is true again.

What happens

  • M2 deliberately treats points left by a pause as frozen. freeze() refuses them (:555) and undo() clears them all (:460), because ARCore may not relocalise to the same world frame on resume.
  • The front capture ignores this. It projects those frozen world positions into the post-resume camera and gives "step back / turn" guidance towards wherever they now land.
  • trackingOk is true, so it captures and saves whatever the camera sees there.

Failure scenario

  • In step 3 the user presses the power button or takes a call, then comes back. Pausing to grab a tape measure is natural in a scan flow.
  • ARCore resumes with a shifted origin. The capture now guides the user to a patch of wall 30 cm to the left of the frame, and auto-captures it.
  • The Review shows a straightened photo of plain wall, which is then saved as the item's front.
  • The same happens if the pause occurs on the Outline step and the user taps Next afterwards.

Fix

  • In startFrontCapture (and in processFrontCapture), refuse when any endpoint has anchor == null. Expose this as a hint, for example CaptureHint.REMEASURE.
  • In ScanArScreen, show "The outline was lost when the app paused. Measure again or skip the photo", with [Measure again] (Undo clears all) and [Skip photo].
  • Test this with a fake: mark the endpoints unanchored and assert that the capture never arms.
  • Add to the device checklist: "pause in step 3 (power button), resume → the photo is refused, not taken".

MINOR

# Where Problem → failure Fix
m1 MeasureSession.kt:776-783, ScanArScreen.kt:363 If the bitmap conversion fails (OOM) while the session is paused, startFrontCapture returns false. _frontCapture stays CAPTURED ("Got it."), Skip is disabled, and the LaunchedEffect never re-arms because capture != null. The user is stuck until Back. On failure, set _frontCapture.value = null before calling startFrontCapture, so the screen's effect re-arms on resume.
m2 ScanArScreen.kt:152-154, MeasureSession.kt:542-547,786 cancelFrontCapture (Back to the outline, Undo, pause) does not cancel a conversion that is already running. A photo arriving 50–200 ms later is still handed to onPhoto, which jumps to Review from the Outline step, or attaches a photo of an outline that was just undone. Keep the conversion Job plus a capture generation number. Cancel it in cancelFrontCapture, and publish only if the generation still matches.
m3 ScanArScreen.kt:142-148,184; MeasureSession.kt:283-296 The Photo step on a new session (Try again, or SceneView re-creating the session) has no endpoints. startFrontCapture fails forever while the card says "Move the phone slowly…". When step == PHOTO && state.pointCount < shape.mode.requiredPoints, call backToOutline() with a short message.
m4 ScanRoute.kt:103, ScanReviewScreen.kt:83, ScanViewModel.kt:188-191 Back or Measure again on Review silently discards the outline, photo, edits and name, with no confirmation. It also works during a save: the step goes to OUTLINE (camera starts), then the save completes and jumps to SAVED. Disable both while saving, and confirm when there are edits, a label or a photo.
m5 ScanViewModel.kt (no SavedStateHandle); :220 (a) Process death on Saved brings the user back to step 1 ("What are you scanning?") for an item that is already saved. (b) ScanEvent.Saved is sent with tryEmit on a no-replay SharedFlow. If no collector is active at that instant, recordCompletedScan is skipped. That errs towards fewer ads, but the cap count drifts. Save savedId and the step in SavedStateHandle, and restore to SAVED. Call recordCompletedScan from the view model's save path (injected callback), or use a Channel.
m6 MeasureSession.kt:611-626 (close), :770-787; ScanViewModel.kt:143-160 Bitmaps are left for the GC on three paths: a published _frontPhoto that was never taken; a conversion that finishes after scope.cancel(); and an onFrontPhoto job cancelled before its withContext block starts, where the finally never runs. With minSdk 24, API 24–25 bitmaps live on the Java heap (about 5 MB each). Recycle _frontPhoto.value in close(). Use try/finally around the conversion result. In the view model, use invokeOnCompletion { if (cancelled) recycle }.
m7 MeasurementComposer.kt:321-338 Nothing requires the BOX top point to be above the base. A top point tapped on the floor next to the box gives "H 1.2 cm ± 1" as a measured box. If it is below the base, boxFace builds the face downwards. Reject the 4th commit when its height above the base is under about 2 cm, or negative, with a "aim at the top" CommitFailure.
m8 MeasurementComposer.kt:330; DimensionPainter.kt:66; ScanComposer.kt:161-162 Three different base references: H is measured from the nearest tapped corner; the painter draws it from the nearest of four corners, including the inferred one; the photo face is lifted from the mean of the three. On an uneven floor the drawn H, measured H and photo height disagree slightly. Use one helper (for example boxFoot) for all three.
m9 MeasureHud.kt:306-313, FreezeOverlay.kt:134-137 BOX readouts are mislabelled. With 3 points the live label uses the W × H format for W × D. With 4 points it shows first edge × second edge × H, while the Outline card may swap W and D (front edge). In freeze-frame, while the top point is being adjusted, H, the value being adjusted, is not shown. Use the BOX branch in FreezeOverlay, use D wording for 3 points, and order the values as the Outline card will.
m10 ScanReviewScreen.kt:100-103, scan_type_tv For a TV the user outlines the outer bezel, but the Review shows it as "Diagonal", in inches with a ±. TVs are sold by visible screen diagonal, so a "55-inch" set reads about 56–57 in. The ± itself is fine. Label it "Outer diagonal (with bezel)", or tell TV users to outline the screen glass.
m11 ScanDraftBuilder.kt:117-124 and ScanDraft The Review shows the engine's rectangle diagonal ±, but ScanDraft has no diagonal, so the Gallery recomputes its own (gallery MAJOR 1). The same item shows two different ± for the diagonal. Add diagonal: Dimension? to ScanDraft, or share one diagonalOf(w, h) that both sides call. Coordinate with B's fix.
m12 values*/strings.xml:276-278 (scan_aim_base_*) "Back-right corner on the floor": that corner is hidden for an appliance against a wall, and "floor" is wrong for a carton on a table. The engine accepts any L-shaped three corners (threeCornerOrder). Change the text to "…a back corner (left or right)" and "on the floor or table" (EN + HI).
m13 FrontCaptureGate.kt:126 One 15° parallel limit for every shape. For a 40 cm carton on the floor, the user must crouch to bring the face within 15°. The homography corrects perspective, and TOO_SMALL already guards resolution. Use a per-shape limit (for example 25–30° for BOX) as a tunable, and add it to the bench test.
m14 ScanArScreen.kt:337-350; ScanReviewScreen.kt:200-202; ScanRoute.kt:157-159 Accessibility: the TILT text (with the angle) is in a polite live region and is re-announced at every 5° step. There are three identical "Edit" buttons with no dimension name. The progress bar has no description. "Something else" appears as both card title and button. Announce on hint changes only, without the angle. Label the buttons "Edit width / height / depth". Add progressBarRangeInfo or a description. Drop the duplicate button text.
m15 MeasureSession.kt:754-760, ImageProjection.kt:58-66,88-94 Hot path, small issues. Capture copies about 1.4 MB of NV21 on the main/render thread, with per-element chroma get (an estimated 5–20 ms hitch once per scan, acceptable). While armed, each frame allocates a quaternion inverse per corner, a withIndex iterator and two status objects. Optional: compute the inverse rotation once per frame. The B1 duplicate skip already halves the calls.
m16 Nv21.kt, FrameReaders.kt:252-278 Odd widths and heights are handled by clamping, and toNv21 honours row and pixel strides. Neither is tested: Nv21Test uses even sizes only, and toNv21 (pixelStride 2, padded rowStride) has no test. Add Nv21.toArgb tests for odd sizes. Extract toNv21 over (ByteBuffer, rowStride, pixelStride) so it can be JVM-tested with padded, interleaved planes.

Verified correct

Area Evidence
BOX maths W × D come from rectangleReading on the three base corners (MeasurementComposer.kt:321-338): convexity, squareness and planarity checks, ROUGH when inconsistent, and the tier floor in quadrature. H uses lengthReading(HEIGHT, foot, top), the vertical component with its own floor. The badge is the worse of the two. calibrated is true only when both are in scope. ScanComposerTest.floorCalibrationDoesNotCoverTheHeight shows that a floor calibration never scales H.
Level-base assumption H is vertical from a base corner. W and D are the base-plane edge lengths. The face is a vertical extrusion of the front base edge. Acceptable for v1 (but see m8).
± never shrinks The composer rounds σ up (gate R1). ScanDraftBuilder.measured takes the ceiling of ±95 to 0.1 mm (:84-88). DisplayedBoundTest now covers boxHeight across contexts and preferences. The Review and Outline cards format from the stored ±95. GalleryRepository.normalised only zeroes estimates.
Tier floor Every BOX dimension goes through UncertaintyModel.length or height (floor in quadrature). The test asserts H ≥ absFloor95.
No calibration in scans ScanArScreen.kt:124 calls setCalibration(null). A new session gets a new UUID. reviewTip maps CALIBRATE to MEASURE_AGAIN. The draft has calibrationFactor = 1.0, and calibrated is always false.
Flat W/H, any tap order ScanComposer.flatGeometry runs Shapes.rectangle on the same three positions, so threeCornerOrder picks the same middle corner as the reading. W is the edge most aligned with world-up × n. Tests cover clockwise taps and taps that go down first.
Diagonal ± The rectangle's own diagonal is used. Its random part is propagated from W and H; the noise of the shared corner s1 is included, which is conservative. Scale and floor are applied once. An edit gives a TYPED or PRESET diagonal with no ± (ScanDraftBuilder.kt:117-124).
Edits and presets An edit becomes Dimension.typed (± 0, "Edited by you"). Preset depth is labelled "estimate", with scan_depth_note. Use measured resets. The badge rates measured values only and is ROUGH on bad geometry. gallery_badge_measured_only is shown once anything is edited.
Projection ImageProjection uses camera.pose (the sensor pose, +X along image rows) and imageIntrinsics (CPU-image pixels). These are the same conventions as DepthPatch, so display rotation never enters. scaledTo matches the acquired image size. TL, TR, BR, BL as seen from the front land clockwise in the non-mirrored image, so Homography.rectify keeps corner 0: no mirroring, and no rotation for vertical faces. Tests include round trips with Ray.fromPixel and DepthPatch.
NV21 V then U (NV21), from planes[2] and planes[1] with their rowStride and pixelStride. Full-range BT.601 fixed-point coefficients, clamped. Odd sizes are clamped safely.
Memory The camera config is limited to a CPU long side ≤ 1280 (SessionSetup.kt:44,90), so the bitmap is ≤ about 5 MB. The rectifier caps its window at 4 MP. Output is ≤ 1024 px.
ARCore threading All Frame, Camera and Image use is inside onFrame (main/session thread). acquireCameraImage is closed with use {} on every path. Only ByteArray, Bitmap and FloatArray leave the thread. Conversion runs on the worker, rectify and JPEG on Dispatchers.Default, and the gallery write on I/O.
Lifecycle Capture is disarmed in onSessionPaused, onSessionCreated, undo, clear and close. The new code never detaches an anchor and never touches the session after close(). closed is checked after the conversion.
Compose throttling FrontCaptureStatus is a data class (5° angle steps, progress in quarters) and is set only when it changes. This breaks only under B1.
Ads Place calls onVisitLeftWithoutAd, which never arms a chance and also drops a pending one. Share stays on Saved and arms nothing. Done and Back after a save call onMeasureLeaving, which arms one chance consumed by Gallery or Home once RESUMED and once no AR screen is composed. Leaving without a save arms nothing. isCurrent() guards double taps. ArScreenAdGuard covers both AR steps, and Saved is non-AR. recordCompletedScan is called once per stored scan: Save is guarded synchronously by saving/savedId on the main thread. Tracker tests: 14.
Measure regression ArCameraView passes the same parameters as before. The MeasureSession.onFrame changes do nothing unless a capture is armed (pose is computed once and reused). The cancelFrontCapture calls set an already-null StateFlow. The BOX branches are reachable only from Scan. The M2 tests still pass.
Strings 45 scan_* keys plus 4 home_* keys in both EN and HI (EN has only app_name extra, which is untranslatable). The format arguments match (%1$d/%2$d, %1$s × %2$s × %3$s, %1$d°).
Gallery contract use ScanDraft, DimensionSource (MEASURED, PRESET, TYPED), GalleryRepository.save (exceptions caught and shown as saveFailed), shareCopy, FrontPhotoRectifier (corner 0 = top-left, aspect = measured W/H) and isValidSide are all used as documented.

Tests missing

  1. FrontCaptureGate with repeated timestamps (B1), and a gap-then-resume sequence.
  2. The front face decided once: outline → capture with the camera moving in between; long-object edge choice (M1); horizontal flat face seen from the other side.
  3. No capture with unanchored points (M2). This needs a small seam: a fake endpoint provider, or extracting the capture state machine from MeasureSession into a JVM class.
  4. No ScanViewModelTest:
    • double Save → one gallery.save and one Saved event;
    • remeasure during a save (m4);
    • onFrontPhoto cancelled, then the bitmap is recycled (m6);
    • onFrontPhoto with no outline;
    • skipPhoto leads to a draft with no JPEG. These are JVM-testable with a fake GalleryRepository, using an interface or a test double.
  5. An end-to-end orientation test across :ar and app: project a known textured world quad with ImageProjection (portrait and landscape sensor poses), rectify it, and check that the output is upright and not mirrored.
  6. Nv21 odd sizes, and toNv21 with padded, interleaved planes (m16).
  7. A BOX case in DisplayedBoundTest with a realistic floor base. The current one puts the base on the wall plane and only exercises the height path.

Add to docs/TESTING.md (device)

  • Auto photo: taken within about 1 s of holding still. Test on a 60 Hz phone and on a 90/120 Hz phone (B1).
  • Box front choice:
    • Finish a 120 cm cabinet standing near its corner, then face the long side: the photo is the long face and W is 120 (M1).
    • The Review photo of a TV or frame is upright in portrait and in landscape.
  • Pause in step 3 (power button), then resume: no photo is taken from stale corners (M2).
  • Low carton on the floor: can it be captured without crouching? Tune MAX_ANGLE_DEG (m13).
  • Steadiness thresholds: with a normal hand-held hold, HOLDING is reached. Tune STEADY_MAX_*.
  • Ads:
    • Save, then Place: no ad.
    • Save, then Share, then Done: at most one ad, over the Gallery.
    • Save, then Back: at most one ad, over Home.
    • Back without a save: never an ad.
    • The interstitial never appears over the camera.
Reviewsdocs/reviews/M3-gate.md · updated 05 Nov 2026, 20:18

M3 gate: Scan → Gallery (Final Reviewer + Judge)

Judge, 2026-11-05. Gate tree: the uncommitted work on top of ccebb01 (Builder A's scan flow, Builder B's gallery, and both fix rounds). I checked it against docs/PLAN.md (M3 row, §4 budgets), HANDOFF.md, docs/research/ui-ux.md §3.3 A–C and §5.2, docs/reviews/M3-scan-review.md and docs/reviews/M3-gallery-review.md. No source was edited.

Verdict: PASS WITH CONDITIONS

All 1 BLOCKER and 5 MAJOR findings from the two reviews are fixed in the code, and each has a test that would have caught it. I found no new BLOCKER or MAJOR.

  • The front photo now captures on repeated SceneView frames.
  • The front face is decided once and travels with the outline.
  • No photo is taken against corners left unanchored by a pause.
  • The diagonal ± is a fully correlated upper bound on both screens.
  • A dimension edit can no longer split the model from its row.
  • Place never shows an ad.

The per-scan budget holds against real encoder bytes and is now a hard check. The APK grew by about 0.15 MB.

Two conditions remain:

  • R1. The Gallery labels a TV's outer (bezel) diagonal as "Diagonal" (scan m10, half fixed).
  • R2. The device checklist must carry the M3 items, especially the v1→v2 upgrade on a phone that already has M2 History rows. The migration has never run on a device.

Both are small. No new review round is needed.

How this was verified

  • Reading. I read every file changed or added since ccebb01 that the review findings and cross-cutting checks touch:
    • :ar: FrontCaptureController, FrontCaptureGate, ScanComposer, ImageProjection, Nv21, and the MeasureSession / MeasurementComposer / FrameReaders diffs.
    • app/scan: all files.
    • app/gallery: GalleryFormatter, plus the parts of GalleryScreen and GalleryItemScreen that matter here.
    • data/gallery: ScanDraft, GalleryItemEntity, the GalleryRepository save / edit / delete / share paths.
    • SpaceViewDatabase and the migration test.
    • AppNavHost, AdsManager, MeasureVisitTracker, AdScreenEffects.
    • The Measure diffs: MeasureViewModel, MeasureHud, FreezeOverlay, MeasureScreen, DimensionPainter.
    • The tests behind each fix.
  • Schema. A script compared data/schemas/…/2.json with MIGRATION_1_2.
    • The gallery_items createSql is identical to the migration string.
    • The index matches.
    • The four measurements columns are nullable with no default, in both the entity and the ALTERs.
    • identityHash 3968e687… in 2.json equals the one in the generated SpaceViewDatabase_Impl (debug and release). So the JSON was re-exported after the in-place v2 change, not edited by hand.
  • Strings. A script compared values/ with values-hi/:
    • EN 295 keys, HI 294. The missing one is app_name, which is untranslatable.
    • No key is missing, and no format argument differs.
    • Four entries are identical on purpose (format-only or licence strings).
  • Sizes.
    • The scan sizes are the numbers printed by GalleryRepositoryTest in the last green run (data/build/test-results/.../GalleryRepositoryTest.xml).
    • The APK is the orchestrator's release build (14:06).
    • I estimated the download with deflate-9 over the APK without armeabi-v7a/x86, the same method as at the M2 gate.
  • Orchestrator build, green:
    • Unit tests: engine 163, ar 83, data 48, app 97, with 0 failures.
    • koverVerify, assembleDebug, assembleRelease, and lintDebug for app, ar and data.
    • I did not run Gradle.

1. Review items (BLOCKER and MAJOR)

# Finding Status Evidence
Scan B1 Auto-capture never fires: SceneView repeats camera timestamps at display rate and the gate reset the hold FIXED FrontCaptureController.kt:84 returns early when timestampNs == lastTs. FrontCaptureGate.kt:120 also ignores dt ≤ 1e-4 without touching motion or the hold, the same rule as TrackingGate. MeasureSession.kt:405 feeds the controller only while armed. Tests: FrontCaptureControllerTest.capturesOnRepeatedFramesAt60And120Hz (each timestamp 1×, 2× and 4×; captured within HOLD_NS + 4 frames) and FrontCaptureGateTest.repeatedCameraFramesStillCapture.
Scan M1 Box front face chosen twice, with the wrong (midpoint) rule FIXED Rule: ScanComposer.frontEdge (ScanComposer.kt:143-162) takes the argmax of the outward normal · direction to the camera. Decided once: MeasureSession.scanOutline (:521-530) decides FaceChoice once per point generation (anchor refinement does not bump the generation). The choice is stored in ScanOutline.choice. startFrontCapture(outline) (:540-546) arms with that outline, and FrontCaptureController only ever builds ScanComposer.face(shape, points, o.choice). FLAT keeps its up vector from outline time. Tests: ScanComposerTest.wardrobeLongFaceWinsNearItsEnd (all four probe positions → edge 0), faceChoiceIsStableWhenTheCameraMoves, horizontalFlatFaceKeepsItsOrientationFromTheOtherSide, FrontCaptureControllerTest.photographsTheOutlinesFace, and the end-to-end FrontPhotoOrientationTest (landscape, portrait and oblique views come out upright and unmirrored).
Scan M2 Photo taken against corners left unanchored by a pause FIXED Arming: MeasureSession.kt:543 arms with pointsAnchored = endpoints.all { it.anchor != null }, and FrontCaptureController.arm (:49) refuses with REMEASURE. While armed: losing anchors disarms (:86). Screen: ScanArScreen.kt:350-399 shows "The outline was lost when the app paused…" with Measure again (clears every point) and Skip photo. Pause also disarms (MeasureSession.onSessionPaused) and drops any conversion in flight. Tests: refusesUnanchoredPoints, losingAnchorsWhileArmedStopsTheCapture.
Gallery M1 TV diagonal ± understated by 20–27%; a ± shown on a typed diagonal FIXED GalleryFormatter.kt:71-79: b_d = (w·b_w + h·b_h)/d (fully correlated, so an upper bound). σ = 0 and "~" when W or H is an estimate. Tests: GalleryFormatterTest.diagonalBoundCoversTheEngineDiagonalForTheReviewCases and diagonalOnAnEstimateHasNoPlusMinus.
Gallery M2 Edit writes the GLB before the row, so the two can diverge FIXED GalleryRepository.kt:244-288: the new model is written under a new name (nextModelName), then one UPDATE sets the dimensions and the model path, then the old file is deleted. All of this is NonCancellable, and the new file is deleted on failure. cleanUp sweeps unreferenced models and regenerates a missing one from the row. Tests: failedDimensionWriteKeepsModelAndRowInAgreement, deathBeforeTheRowUpdateLeavesOldPairAndSweepsTheNewModel, deathAfterTheRowUpdateSweepsTheOldModel, repeatedEditsInTheSameMillisecondGetDistinctModels.
Gallery M3 Place on the Saved screen would use up the after-save interstitial FIXED (by Builder A) AppNavHost.kt:141-146: Place calls ads.onVisitLeftWithoutAd(entry.id), which goes to MeasureVisitTracker.leaveWithoutAd (:68-74). That closes the visit without arming and drops any pending chance. AdsManager.onVisitLeftWithoutAd also cancels a pending show job. Tests: MeasureVisitTrackerTest "scan saved then Place in my room never arms an interstitial" and "leaving without an ad also drops an already armed chance".

MINORs spot-checked

# Item Status
Scan m1 Stuck on "Got it." after a conversion fails FIXED: the failure disarms, clears the status and re-arms (MeasureSession.kt:798-805).
Scan m2 Late photo after Back or Undo FIXED: every arm, disarm and capture bumps a generation, and the conversion Job is cancelled in cancelFrontCapture. The photo is published only if the generation still matches. ScanViewModel.onFrontPhoto also recycles a photo that arrives when the step is no longer PHOTO.
Scan m3 Photo step on a new session FIXED: ScanArScreen.kt:155-157 goes back to the outline.

A flow that was saved comes back on Saved; one still measuring restarts its outline (anchors cannot survive). onScanSaved is called from the save itself (AppNavHost.kt:117). Tested (processDeathOnSavedRestoresSaved, processDeathWhileMeasuringRestartsTheOutline, doubleSaveStoresOnceAndCountsOnce).
